{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Wt5hS4pLw7c3q56AEcQEq6","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→// ============================================================\n     2→// tools/outreachEvents.js — Learning-loop event helpers\n     3→//\n     4→// Sprint 2 central sink for granular outreach telemetry. Every\n     5→// touch-point (email sent, pixel open, GHL stage change, webhook\n     6→// bounce, landing creation) flows through logOutreachEvent so the\n     7→// Scout tool, nightly consolidator, and circuit breaker can mine\n     8→// performance patterns per niche × metro × channel.\n     9→//\n    10→// Design rules:\n    11→//   • Silent-fail when Supabase or LEARNING_ENABLED is off — the\n    12→//     send loop MUST NEVER be blocked by a missing event write.\n    13→//   • Agents and dispatchers call log/get — no schema knowledge\n    14→//     leaks out.\n    15→// ============================================================\n    16→\n    17→import { Tool } from '../lib/AgentRuntime.js';\n    18→import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase.js';\n    19→import { logger as rootLogger } from '../lib/logger.js';\n    20→\n    21→const log = rootLogger.child({ module: 'outreachEvents' });\n    22→\n    23→export const LEARNING_ENABLED = () =>\n    24→  process.env.LEARNING_ENABLED === 'true';\n    25→\n    26→const VALID_CHANNELS = new Set([\n    27→  'email', 'whatsapp', 'sms', 'phone', 'pixel', 'ghl', 'landing',\n    28→]);\n    29→const VALID_EVENT_TYPES = new Set([\n    30→  'sent', 'opened', 'clicked', 'replied', 'bounced', 'read',\n    31→  'visited', 'stage_change', 'scroll_50', 'cta_click', 'form_submit',\n    32→  'unsubscribed', 'delivered', 'failed',\n    33→]);\n    34→\n    35→/**\n    36→ * Insert a single outreach event. Never throws — any failure is\n    37→ * logged at warn level and returns null so the caller can keep\n    38→ * going. When LEARNING_ENABLED !== 'true' this is a no-op.\n    39→ *\n    40→ * @param {object} params\n    41→ * @param {string|null} params.leadId     - leads.id (nullable; e.g. pixel ping before lead resolves)\n    42→ * @param {string}     params.brandId    - brands.id (required; rejects silently if missing)\n    43→ * @param {string}     params.channel    - one of VALID_CHANNELS\n    44→ * @param {string}     params.eventType  - one of VALID_EVENT_TYPES\n    45→ * @param {object}     [params.metadata] - free-form jsonb payload\n    46→ * @param {string|null}[params.messageId] - SMTP/Resend message-id / GHL contactId / similar\n    47→ * @returns {Promise<object|null>} inserted row or null\n    48→ */\n    49→export async function logOutreachEvent({\n    50→  leadId = null,\n    51→  brandId,\n    52→  channel,\n    53→  eventType,\n    54→  metadata = {},\n    55→  messageId = null,\n    56→} = {}) {\n    57→  if (!LEARNING_ENABLED()) return null;\n    58→  if (!supabase) {\n    59→    log.warn('logOutreachEvent skip: supabase not configured');\n    60→    return null;\n    61→  }\n    62→  if (!brandId) {\n    63→    log.warn('logOutreachEvent skip: brandId required');\n    64→    return null;\n    65→  }\n    66→  if (!VALID_CHANNELS.has(channel)) {\n    67→    log.warn('logOutreachEvent skip: invalid channel', { channel });\n    68→    return null;\n    69→  }\n    70→  if (!VALID_EVENT_TYPES.has(eventType)) {\n    71→    log.warn('logOutreachEvent skip: invalid eventType', { eventType });\n    72→    return null;\n    73→  }\n    74→\n    75→  try {\n    76→    const { data, error } = await supabase\n    77→      .from('outreach_events')\n    78→      .insert({\n    79→        lead_id:    leadId,\n    80→        brand_id:   brandId,\n    81→        channel,\n    82→        event_type: eventType,\n    83→        metadata:   metadata || {},\n    84→        message_id: messageId,\n    85→      })\n    86→      .select()\n    87→      .single();\n    88→    if (error) {\n    89→      log.warn('logOutreachEvent insert error', { error: error.message });\n    90→      return null;\n    91→    }\n    92→    return data;\n    93→  } catch (err) {\n    94→    log.warn('logOutreachEvent threw', { error: err?.message });\n    95→    return null;\n    96→  }\n    97→}\n    98→\n    99→/**\n   100→ * Fetch recent outreach events with optional filters. Silent-fail\n   101→ * returns an empty array.\n   102→ */\n   103→export async function getRecentEvents(\n   104→  { brandId, leadId, channel, eventType, sinceHours } = {},\n   105→  limit = 50,\n   106→) {\n   107→  if (!supabase) return [];\n   108→  try {\n   109→    let q = supabase\n   110→      .from('outreach_events')\n   111→      .select('*')\n   112→      .order('occurred_at', { ascending: false })\n   113→      .limit(limit);\n   114→\n   115→    if (brandId)   q = q.eq('brand_id', brandId);\n   116→    if (leadId)    q = q.eq('lead_id', leadId);\n   117→    if (channel)   q = q.eq('channel', channel);\n   118→    if (eventType) q = q.eq('event_type', eventType);\n   119→    if (Number.isFinite(sinceHours)) {\n   120→      const since = new Date(Date.now() - sinceHours * 3600_000).toISOString();\n   121→      q = q.gte('occurred_at', since);\n   122→    }\n   123→\n   124→    const { data, error } = await q;\n   125→    if (error) {\n   126→      log.warn('getRecentEvents error', { error: error.message });\n   127→      return [];\n   128→    }\n   129→    return data || [];\n   130→  } catch (err) {\n   131→    log.warn('getRecentEvents threw', { error: err?.message });\n   132→    return [];\n   133→  }\n   134→}\n   135→\n   136→/**\n   137→ * Aggregate send/open/click/reply/bounce counts and rates for a\n   138→ * (niche, metro, channel) slice over a window. Joins leads to\n   139→ * filter by industry/metro_area (ILIKE, both optional).\n   140→ *\n   141→ * Returns:\n   142→ *   { sends, opens, clicks, replies, bounces,\n   143→ *     open_rate, click_rate, reply_rate, bounce_rate,\n   144→ *     sample_size, low_confidence }\n   145→ *\n   146→ * Silent-fail returns zero-value shape so the Scout tool can still\n   147→ * parse JSON and fall back to default GATE thresholds.\n   148→ *\n   149→ * Allows an injected `client` for tests.\n   150→ */\n   151→export async function getHistoricalPerformance({\n   152→  niche,\n   153→  metro,\n   154→  channel = 'email',\n   155→  windowDays = 30,\n   156→  client,\n   157→} = {}) {\n   158→  const emptyShape = {\n   159→    sends: 0, opens: 0, clicks: 0, replies: 0, bounces: 0,\n   160→    open_rate: 0, click_rate: 0, reply_rate: 0, bounce_rate: 0,\n   161→    sample_size: 0, low_confidence: true,\n   162→  };\n   163→\n   164→  const db = client || supabase;\n   165→  if (!db) return emptyShape;\n   166→\n   167→  const since = new Date(Date.now() - windowDays * 86_400_000).toISOString();\n   168→\n   169→  try {\n   170→    // We pull rows joined with leads(industry, metro_area) so we can\n   171→    // filter on a per-tenant slice. // path doesn't always set these on the SMTP transport).\n   700→        await db\n   701→          .from('campaign_enriched_data')\n   702→          .update({\n   703→            email_sent_at:   new stats.errors++;\n   718→      logger.warn('dispatchApprovedEmail send threw', {\n   719→        business: lead.business_name, error: err.message,\n   720→      });\n   721→    }\n   722→\n   723→ > 0) logger.info('dispatchApprovedEmail summary', stats);\n   729→  return stats;\n   730→}\n   731→\n   732→// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n   733→// Multichannel
